config\\n\\ndef get_token():\\n    cfg = load_config()\\n    return cfg.get('GITEA_API_TOKEN')\\n\\ndef get_template_repos(token):\\n    url = \\\"%s/api/v1/repos/search?limit=50\\\" % GITEA_URL\\n json.loads(resp.read())\\n            repos = data.get('data', data) if isinstance(data, dict) else data\\n            return [r for r in repos if r.get('template', False)]\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log(\\\"API error: %s\\\" % e)\\n        return []\\n\\ndef sync_template(repo_info):\\n    owner = repo_info['owner']['login']\\n    name = repo_info['name'].lower()\\n    bare_path = filepath = parts\\n        mode, objtype, objhash = meta.split()\\n        if objtype == 'blob':\\n            entries.append((mode, objhash, filepath))\\n\\n    if not entries:\\n        log(\\\"  no # 👑 ROOT OBTIDO — Máquina comprometida por completo

**root.txt: `39c4fb98821da1f8cef7f9cfc18618d5`**

A cadeia completa funcionou: o timer root executou o sync com path traversal → escreveu nossa chave pública em `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` (e o SUID `/tmp/rootbash` de backup) → login SSH direto como root.

**Evidências coletadas:** user.txt `a04068bf2ba42508207d4f566b6638eb` · root.txt `39c4fb98821da1f8cef7f9cfc18618d5` · SUID `/tmp/rootbash` · chave SSH root ativa.
